- **diplomatic-policy-evidence-pack**: 외교 업무의 외교정책 근거 팩 절차. 내부 public-policy-evidence-pack capability를 사용하며 draft-only 경계를 지킵니다. ([vertexsquad/k-gov-skills](https://github.com/vertexsquad/k-gov-skills/tree/HEAD/domains/%EC%99%B8%EA%B5%90/skills/diplomatic-policy-evidence-pack) · ★3)
- **diplomatic-pressure-with-respect**: Use when bringing rebellious rulers back into submission without military force. Honors ancestral graves, selects envoys with prior relationships, provides face-saving language, and removes symbols of independent sovereignty. - **diplomatic-risk-assessment**: Use when evaluating whether to enter a powerful party's territory or accept an asymmetric alliance. Applies the earth puppet vs. wooden puppet parable to assess exit options, leverage once inside, and the risk of being trapped with no recourse in a 'tiger and wolf' (虎狼) state. ([baojie/shiji-kb](https://github.com/baojie/shiji-kb/tree/HEAD/kg/ontology/ontology-v1/skus/procedural/skill_151) · ★3.8k)
- **diplomatic-state-assessment**: Use when assessing foreign state stability, predicting political upheaval, or advising allies on self-preservation. Follows Ji Zha's diplomatic mission model across Qi, Zheng, Wei, and Jin to evaluate leadership and identify safe havens. ([baojie/shiji-kb](https://github.com/baojie/shiji-kb/tree/HEAD/kg/ontology/ontology-v1/skus/procedural/skill_064) · ★3.8k)

([baojie/shiji-kb](https://github.com/baojie/shiji-kb/tree/HEAD/kg/ontology/ontology-v1/skus/procedural/skill_042) · ★3.8k)

- **dips**: Use this whenever a response could benefit from richer visualization, guided interaction, or a touch of fun — pickers, calculators, sliders, mini explorers, charts, animated demos, choose-your-own-adventure prompts, anything that lands better as a hydrating widget than as plain prose. Dips are ephemeral `shtml` code blocks rendered inline in chat (no state persistence, lick-only). Reach for them generously: every interactive moment the user gets is a moment they don't have to type a clarifying message. For persistent dashboards, editors, or multi-page apps use sprinkles instead. ([ai-ecoverse/slicc](https://github.com/ai-ecoverse/slicc/tree/HEAD/packages/vfs-root/workspace/skills/dips) · ★30)

- **dips2-portal**: ドローン情報基盤システム2.0 (Dips 2.0 / OSSポータル) を操作するためのプレイブック。機体登録、飛行許可・承認申請、飛行計画通報、飛行実績報告、事故等報告、技能証明、機体認証の各サブシステムでブラウザ自動化を行う際に参照する。Use when the user mentions "Dips", "DIPS 2.0", "ドローン", "ossportal.dips.mlit.go.jp", "飛行許可", "飛行計画通報", "機体登録", "飛行実績報告", "ドローン申請", or related drone regulation tasks. - **dipstick-maintenance-schedule**: Free taster from Dipstick. Builds a maintenance schedule for one car from the user's owner's manual intervals (or typical ranges labeled "check your manual", never invented intervals for a model), picks the normal or severe schedule, works out what's due next by miles and date, and adds a 10-minute monthly check. Use when someone asks when their next oil change is, what maintenance their car needs or what's due at a mileage. - **diptrace-evidence-capture**: RAG-backed. Collect reviewable DipTrace evidence with native headless PCB acceptance when supported, open/save/close for all four editors and supported native capture; for unsupported actions guide an operator through quarantined source/open-save/re-export capture, dry-run ingest, MCP validation, explicit confirmation, and metadata recording. Use for native round-trip, refill/DRC, XML re-export or recording evidence; «нативная приёмка платы», «прогони через DipTrace», «сними свидетельство round-trip».
